Sequential Analysis

Question: Where should we increase the armor plating?

Abraham Wald (1902-1950)

boardwork

Sequential Probability Ratio Test (SPRT)

Parameters

p0 <- 0.05     # acceptable defect rate under H0
p1 <- 0.15     # unacceptable defect rate under H1
alpha <- 0.05
beta  <- 0.10
n_max   <- 200        # max sample size allowed

Simulate one run of the SPRT

SPRT thresholds

# SPRT thresholds
A <- (1 - beta) / alpha
B <- beta / (1 - alpha)
logA <- log(A)
logB <- log(B)
logA
[1] 2.890372
logB
[1] -2.251292

Threshold crossing

Many Simulations

Write a function:

sprt_test <- function(p_true, p0, p1, logA, logB, n_max = 200) {
  x <- rbinom(n_max, size = 1, prob = p_true)

  log_lr <- cumsum(
    x * log(p1 / p0) +
      (1 - x) * log((1 - p1) / (1 - p0))
  )

  stop_reject <- which(log_lr >= logA)[1]
  stop_accept <- which(log_lr <= logB)[1]

  # Determine which boundary is hit first
  if (!is.na(stop_reject) && (is.na(stop_accept) || stop_reject < stop_accept)) {
    n_used <- stop_reject
    decision <- "reject_H0"
    reject <- 1
  } else if (!is.na(stop_accept) && (is.na(stop_reject) || stop_accept < stop_reject)) {
    n_used <- stop_accept
    decision <- "accept_H0"
    reject <- 0
  } else {
    # No boundary hit by n_max: force terminal decision by sign of log LR
    n_used <- n_max
    if (log_lr[n_max] > 0) {
      decision <- "reject_H0"
      reject <- 1
    } else {
      decision <- "accept_H0"
      reject <- 0
    }
  }

  list(
    decision = decision,
    reject = reject,
    n_used = n_used,
    x = x[1:n_used],
    log_lr = log_lr[1:n_used]
  )
}

n_sims <- 1000
sprt_res_h1 <- replicate(n_sims,
                      sprt_test(p_true = p1, p0, p1, logA, logB, n_max),
                      simplify = FALSE)
sprt_res_h1
